AUTHORITY AND REPAIR SUMMARY
Verified instruction boundaries used by the plan:
- 004DAFAC..004DAFC7: native mode flag; JE skips letterbox width/height writes.
- 004DB083..004DB0A6: conditional width and height initialization.
- 00B6CD24..: original 1280 width; renderer/default-group aspect calls.
- 00B6D849..00B6D8B8: both positive dimensions use requested extent; otherwise default extent.
- 00B6D1A8..00B6D1E0: native requested-size versus square power-of-two storage policy.
- 00706E80..00706E8A: complete width getter; x87 float return, no stack arguments.
- 00706E90..00706F1C: native aspect-height getter calls shared width getter.
- 00712B7D..00712C15: native conditional input conversion through display/menu-width ratio.
- 00EE8490/00EE84B0: color-buffer dimensions through group virtuals +8C/+90.
- Native terminal XML and list-item prefab: equal font selection, different clipping boundaries.
Interior range starts, invalid instructions, and decompilations from non-entry addresses in exploratory sections are not patch authority.
Plan: shared max(1280, default-group width) resolver for sizing splices and menu-width getter; retain native predicate, input conversion, aspect, padding, and effects. No production edit, game run, test, build, deployment, or commit was performed by this static investigation.
The exact installed revision, globals, rectangles, and final image in the reported frame are not supplied by static inspection. Code-contract defects are proven; visual correction is not.

REPAIR SOURCE REVIEW (STATIC ONLY)
Source: psycho-engine-fixes/src/mods/engine_fixes/resolution_fixes.rs
Five owned write descriptors: B6CD2C (19-byte context, seven changed), B6CEC3 (same), 706E80 (eleven-byte replacement entry), 700AB2 (six bytes), 700ACC (six bytes).
A03C60 predicate and 712BA1 conditional branch are now required native contexts, with no write descriptor. All three direct clip-selection branches and the native aspect-height helper are required unchanged contexts.
Both sizing bridges and the float getter call one ECX-renderer/EAX-u32 resolver through renderer virtual C8 and group virtual 8C(index zero). The original native getters have plain RET and RET 4 respectively and use no x87 instructions.
Sizing splice: PUSHFD/PUSHAD, ECX=EDI, call resolver, [EBP]=EAX, POPAD/POPFD/RET. Stack and general registers restored; native x87 state untouched.
Float getter replacement: entry JMP retains original return address; PUSHFD/PUSHAD; native renderer accessor; shared resolver; zero-extended eight-byte integer temporary; FILD/FSTP-f32/FLD-f32; remove temporary; POPAD/POPFD/RET. Net x87 stack effect is one float return, as in the native getter.
No new persistent renderer/target pointer, resource owner, TLS, worker, imports, config layout, event lifecycle, or repeated code-write mechanism is introduced at source level. Existing two lazy owners remain; patch descriptor count decreases and required native contexts increase. No generated artifact or import/layout result is inferred.
Native construction, binding, UV/viewport, capability padding, release, and reset paths are retained. The automated-target terminal path requests type 31 per shared draw and releases it afterward. Cached-target and physical image behavior remain unqualified.
No build, test, game/debug run, deployment, or commit was performed for this implementation. Formatting and source/diff inspection do not establish runtime or image correctness.
